# TODO-33 turn 3 report (2026-10-07)

This report supersedes turn 2's active-run status. The interrupted turn-2 folder was neither reused nor deleted.

## Outcome

**Stopped at BD-4: the single-seed timing pilot extrapolates to 90.720288 serial hours, exceeding the author's 12-hour limit.** No validation or confirmation seed was used. No BD-3 pass verdict is issued. No tolerance was loosened. Selection and pilot commands both finished before this turn ended.

The frozen E4 iteration count is **400**. Selection used the exact BG_SELECTION16 seeds below, four acquisitions per condition per seed (N=64 per condition, 16 independent transported-map clusters). All 448 conditions were run for E0 and all five E4 candidates. The comparison used 75 conditions independently meeting angular association for every candidate; 75 regimes also met the predeclared conditional trust/association screen and were pinned for prospective validation.

Selection seeds: 530001, 634730, 739459, 844188, 948917, 1053646, 1158375, 1263104, 1367833, 1472562, 1577291, 1682020, 1786749, 1891478, 1996207, 2100936.

Candidates minimize the largest norm of the mean paired signed-vector excess on the SAME common association-valid comparison set, with ties to fewer iterations. No pair-resolution rule was transferred. Selection means and their seed uncertainty are exploratory; a conditional binomial screen and a selected point estimate do not establish a detector or calibration-population guarantee.

## Frozen scope, targets and uncertainty procedure

Pin: `samples/evidence/background-shape/pinned-v1.json`, SHA-256 `3c44d28d8c3484a2ae89d7a272cec492377120d8a9da9af7c8de28556aa94c24`. It records selection seeds/repeats, candidates, common association regimes, qualified regimes, every regime's target, iteration count, request and seed hashes, and the bootstrap rule. The pin was written before the timing pilot and before any validation acquisition.

Main recipe: four edge cases, open window, bare bound, cyclic and non-cyclic; 10/60 s; source levels 25/50/100/250/500/1000 pilot-normalized counts and default 1 MBq; field 0/0.05/0.10/0.20 microSv/h. Expected source counts vary with the independent source MC rate: they are not forced to the nominal label. Ambient truth and source-free calibration use separate 500,000-history streams per head/seed; source maps use 1,000,000 biased photons per case/seed. Ideal and field acquisitions share a source Poisson realization and add independent exact ambient Poisson noise. The outer seed retains all calibration and across-condition dependence.

Targets derive from step = rank*cellPitch*z/(maskSpacing*48), then radial target = sqrt(2)*step: a one-step-per-axis engineering cell-equivalence convention, not physical detector accuracy. No accuracy tolerance is borrowed. Conditional angular association uses one element, atan(cellPitch/maskSpacing).

| Case | z / x, mm | Grid step, mm | sqrt(2) target, mm |
|---|---|---:|---:|
| lab | 160 / 8 | 0.38888889 | 0.54997194 |
| head | 155 / 8 | 0.41098485 | 0.58122035 |
| head1m | 1000 / 52.408 | 2.6515152 | 3.7498087 |
| head5m | 5000 / 262.04 | 13.257576 | 18.749043 |

Prospective qualification repeats the association and jointly trusted-association conditional one-sided Clopper-Pearson lower95 >=0.95 screen on validation, without expanding the pinned regimes. Shared calibration uncertainty is separately reported at the seed-cluster level; conditional binomial intervals alone are not a population guarantee. The existing gate Search and AB-11 rounded threshold comparison are unchanged. No E4 trust guarantee or ambient-dose interpretation is added.

The simultaneous bias procedure is fixed before validation: divide each paired signed vector by that condition's fixed geometric target; center the seed-cluster vectors; resample whole seeds together across all pinned regimes 10,000 times with statistical seed 131071; take the higher empirical 95th percentile of the maximum norm of the resampled mean deviation. Each condition's upper bound is its observed mean-vector norm plus this common dimensionless radius times its own target. This is approximate bootstrap coverage and is explicitly recorded as such. No validation-dependent rescaling or tolerance change is permitted.

The four-repeat selection choice derives from 0.05^(1/64)=0.9542702976692375 for zero failures among 64 conditionally independent draws; 59 all-success draws are the minimum to exceed 0.95. It is a selection screen, not precision evidence. The appendix reports outer-seed standard errors; RMS standard errors there use the delta approximation SE(MSE)/(2*RMS), not an exact bound.

## Timing and BD-4 decision

Selection per-seed elapsed seconds: 245.29181, 251.51129, 252.40869, 239.77962, 248.9633, 252.34036, 261.21596, 255.02394, 253.28247, 306.64023, 267.09651, 260.73455, 254.85838, 268.67251, 272.40184, 274.51248. Sum of worker elapsed times = 4164.7339 s; mean = 260.29587 s, descriptive seed-time SD=15.700533 s and SE=3.9251333 s. The single selection driver used four workers; these are measured per-worker elapsed times under contention, not uncontended serial predictions. Approximate invocation wall time from its UTC start and latest done-file timestamp was 1,081.103 s (18.0184 min), with about one-second start-timestamp granularity; this is not a pinned performance guarantee.

Pilot seed 530001, 448 conditions x 16 acquisitions (N=7168 condition acquisitions), one worker, frozen iterations [400]. Elapsed = 557.30644 s. Pilot physical answers are timing-only, one outer map cluster, and have no estimable outer-seed uncertainty; their provenance-bound raw sums remain in the scratch run. They are not validation.

Extrapolation = 32 * (ambient-map seconds + remaining pilot seconds * 300 / pilot repeats); setup conservatively scaled. Estimate 326593.04 s = 90.720288 h against the prescribed 43,200 s (12 h) limit. Setup outside ambient transport is conservatively scaled with repeats, so this is an upper estimate, not a measured full-run runtime. A single pilot provides no seed-to-seed timing confidence bound or speedup guarantee. This main-stage estimate excludes the extra BD-7 workload.

The same one-seed pilot command additionally measured 24 sensitivity base conditions x 16 acquisitions. Sensitivity timing = 368.64224 s; conservative 32*300/16 scaling predicts 61.440374 extra serial hours, total main plus sensitivity 152.16066 h. No validation seed was substituted into this timing run. These one-cluster sensitivity observations are timing-only, not the mandated 32 x 300 sensitivity evidence. The BD-4 stopping decision uses the main-stage estimate, independently of this added cost.

Options for the author: authorize the estimated serial budget; reduce a declared stage-1 matrix/repeat budget and revise its evidence/interval scope before using validation seeds; or authorize a separately timed parallel pilot and explicitly revise the serial-budget restriction. Recommendation: the parallel pilot while preserving the pinned physics, seed/repeat and statistical scope. It must measure the prospective worker count rather than promise an unmeasured speedup. Do not silently cut conditions, reduce validation repeats, change iterations, use confirmation seeds, or claim qualification.

This turn changes seed catalog/pin; generator-only VV hashes; BackgroundShapeRecipe.cs (Z/ideal statistics, runtime pin checks); Program.cs (sensitivity mode); new BackgroundSensitivityRecipe.cs; background aggregator (explicit failure counts, Z, ideal vectors, normalized simultaneous procedure, pin checks, sensitivity phase); new sensitivity manifest family; BackgroundDecodingTests.cs (null serialization assertion); and two additional Python checks (geometric target scaling, exact disjoint seed lists). No Studio source, reference plan/review, Findings or historical evidence result is edited.

BD-7 recipe is prepared: at t=60 s, F=.20 microSv/h, S=250/1000/default for four cases and both cyclic settings (24 base conditions), 32 locked seeds x 300; independently measured 600/3600 s source-free maps; E5/E6 scale offsets -50/-25/-10/+10/+25/+50%; wrong front-only shape on bare truth as an explicitly labelled diagnostic after metadata refusal. Its wrong shape uses the independent bare rate to isolate shape mismatch. The finite count rate uncertainty is sqrt(calibrationCount)/calibrationDuration, conditional on its map; map population uncertainty remains outer-seed variation. No sensitivity outcome is inferred if BD-4 stops execution.

.NET tests: 799 passed /28 skipped before TODO-33 ->815 passed /28 skipped; no failures. Gcam.Tests 465->481; Studio 211 unchanged; Studio.Services 108 passed/7 skipped; headless UI oracles 15 passed/20 desktop skips; render 0 passed/1 skip. No GUI was launched. Python evidence tests: turn 2 had 33 passed/1 skipped ->turn 3 has 35 passed/1 skipped (36 discovered). Seven background Python tests pass. Solution release build and probe release build pass; calibration --release passes. The two pre-existing Studio xUnit warnings are unchanged.

Arithmetic expectations remain exact where construction/order is identical: null-path images/estimates, omitted null serialization, normalization by exact power-of-two scaling, signed E6, E5 fixed b and B=0, beta=0, empty images and metadata refusal. The deterministic scalar test uses four pixels, three source columns, N=200 counts, no MC seed. Float u=2^-24, gamma(k)=ku/(1-ku); one-step relative gamma(22)=1.311303904574269e-6, conservation N*gamma(26)=0.000309944633159665 counts over 60 comparisons. Likelihood tolerance is twice [N*(-log(1-gamma(26)))+predictedTotal*gamma(26)], 0.0012397790129660457 at predictedTotal=N. All 16 new tests pass; these small-matrix roundoff bounds are not arbitrary-matrix precision guarantees.

## Proposed records (exact wording; not applied)

Findings theme 67:

"67. Background shape decoding retains counting noise. Opt-in E4 jointly fits a nonnegative background amplitude outside the pixel-area source grid; E5 uses a declared fixed background mean; E6 subtracts signed counts without clipping. The default null correction preserves existing decoding. On 16 disjoint selection seeds x 4 acquisitions per condition, the single-source iteration rule selected 400 from 60/120/240/400/800 over 75 common association-valid conditions; 75 prospective trust/association regimes are pinned. These selection errors, ideal costs and seed uncertainties are measured, not qualified precision. A one-selection-seed timing pilot of the frozen stage-1 recipe took 557.30644 s and extrapolated to 90.720288 serial hours; the 12-hour rule stopped validation. No stage-1 validation or sensitivity result is released without its complete locked ensemble. The raw AB-12 results remain unchanged. E4 has no pair-resolution, ambient-dose or new gate guarantee."

EV-35:

"EV-35 - Background-aware single-source decoding, selection only; qualification pending. The 16-seed x 4 selection chose E4 at 400 iterations and pinned its regimes/targets. All 448 open-bare edge conditions, cyclic and non-cyclic, have signed errors, paired vector excess, RMS, association, unchanged gate Z/outcomes, fitted amplitude and independent calibration rate with seed uncertainty in the selection summary. The timing pilot predicts 90.720288 serial hours for 32 seeds x 300; a 12-hour budget exceedance stops validation. Deterministic arithmetic checks pass, but selected means are not a precision guarantee and no BD-3 pass is released without locked validation. E5/E6 are explicit known-scale modes. No E4 pair or trust claim is transferred from prior MLEM/search evidence."

EV-01 pointer: "Retained raw precision numbers are unchanged. Background-corrected single-source selection and pending qualification are tracked separately in EV-35; they do not replace this evidence."

EV-12 pointer: "Retained noise/count thresholds and estimator scope are unchanged. E4 ideal cost and corrected count regimes are measured separately in EV-35; existing count thresholds are not transferred to E4."

EV-34 pointer: "The raw AB-12 background pull remains. Opt-in shape correction, residual counting noise and calibration scope are tracked in EV-35; the present selection is not a corrected-precision qualification."

LIM-10: "Shape correction removes an expected spatial term, not realized counting noise. E4 may fit source structure into its nuisance amplitude even with no field; that amplitude is not ambient dose. E5/E6 require a declared known scale. A finite source-free calibration contributes shared shape/rate uncertainty; head response, dimensions, window, field distribution and bound must match. Metadata checks declared identity, not physical truth. Bare/front-only transported maps remain bounds rather than housing calibrations. The unchanged AB-11 gate has no new E4 trust guarantee; E4 has no pair-resolution claim. Report ideal cost, association failures, scale/shape sensitivities and outer-seed uncertainty. The timing-limited selection does not release detector accuracy or corrected precision."

D-52: "Use E4 joint-background pixel-area MLEM as an opt-in single-source correction, keeping the fitted amplitude separate from source cells. Expose E5 fixed-mean MLEM and E6 signed known-scale correlation explicitly. Default null preserves the existing path; no E4 pair claim is inherited."

D-53: "Fit scale from the image for E4; report an independent source-free calibration rate as a cross-check. A dose counter observing source plus ambient is not a subtraction scale. Require explicit matching response/window/bound/field metadata and measure finite-calibration and deliberate scale/shape sensitivities."

D-54: "Use the sqrt(2) grid-step convention only in independently association- and trust-valid pinned regimes. Judge paired signed-vector excess with the predeclared simultaneous seed-cluster bootstrap, recording approximate coverage. Pin iterations, regimes, targets and procedure before validation; record failures rather than widen a tolerance or claim detector accuracy."

D-55: "Stage 1 is open-window, bare-bound, four edge cases, all declared source/field/time levels and both cyclic settings, using 32 locked seeds x 300. Complete selection and pinning first; run one timing pilot, then stop for an author budget decision above 12 extrapolated serial hours. Deferred windows/bounds/centers and unexecuted sensitivities remain pending, and a completed selection is not qualification."
